(SELECT bj.id,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.parentJobId IS NOT NULL) 
THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS totalChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN 
(cj.state = 'SUCCEEDED' OR cj.state = 'DELETED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 
succeededChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'FAILED') 
THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failedChildCount,              MAX(cj.updatedAt) 
as lastChildUpdatedAt         FROM jobrunr_jobs cj RIGHT JOIN 
jobrunr_jobs bj ON bj.id = cj.parentJobId         WHERE bj.isBatch = 1 
AND bj.state  IN ('PROCESSED','FAILED')        GROUP BY bj.id) bjs,     
 jobrunr_jobs j   WHERE j.id = bjs.id and (bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt IS 
NULL OR bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt >= TIMESTAMP '2025-05-01 16:57:27.128' 
OR bjs.totalChildCount = bjs.succeededChildCount)
